    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. {
  "task_type": "diag:dcol_deberta_003",
  "project": "recursive_gen_depth_est",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断 dcol_deberta_003 CUDA OOM 失败\n\n### 背景\ndcol_deberta_003 在 gpu-6000pro-2 的 cuda:1 上提交，25秒内 OOM 崩溃。错误信息：\n```\nCUDA OOM: Tried to allocate 512.00 MiB. GPU 0 has a total capacity of 94.97 GiB of which 310.25 MiB is free. Process 239543 has 57.35 GiB memory in use.\n```\n\n注意：CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 时，torch 看到的 \"GPU 0\" = 物理 cuda:1。\nPID 239543 不是 dcol_003 的 PID（240552），很可能是僵尸进程占了 cuda:1 显存。\n\n这是 DCOL 第三次失败：v1 embedding collapse, v2 OOM(GPU竞争), v3 又 OOM(僵尸进程)。\n\n### 你要做的事\n\n1. **SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2 检查当前 GPU 状态**:\n   ```\n   nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,memory.total --format=csv,noheader\n   nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=pid,gpu_uuid,used_memory --format=csv,noheader\n   ```\n\n2. **检查 PID 239543 是否还存在**:\n   ```\n   ps -p 239543 -o pid,ppid,user,cmd --no-headers 2>/dev/null || echo \"PID_DEAD\"\n   ```\n\n3. **读 dcol_deberta_003 的日志**:\n   ```\n   cat ~/runs/recursive_gen_depth_est/dcol_deberta_003.log\n   ```\n   如果文件不存在，试 /root/runs/recursive_gen_depth_est/dcol_deberta_003.log\n\n4. **检查 tmux sessions 里是否有残留**:\n   ```\n   tmux ls 2>/dev/null\n   ```\n\n5. **如果发现僵尸进程占用 cuda:1，kill 它**:\n   - 先确认 PID 不属于 gpt2xl_topk50_001（PID 211326, cuda:0）\n   - 确认是残留进程后 kill -9\n   - kill 后再次 nvidia-smi 验证 cuda:1 已释放\n\n6. **验证 dcol_trainer.py 的 GPU 设置**:\n   ```\n   head -100 /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/src/dcol_trainer.py | grep -i \"device\\|cuda\\|gpu\"\n   ```\n   确认代码是否正确使用 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 映射后的 GPU 0（而非硬编码某个 GPU ID）。\n\n### 产出\n在回复中清晰说明：\n- 根因（僵尸进程是什么、从哪来的）\n- cuda:1 当前是否已清空可用\n- dcol_trainer.py 的 GPU 设置是否正确\n- 建议下一步（v4 需要改什么）",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ]
}
